# Questions catalog (candidate v2)

GENERATED by `final/_work/questions-catalog.mjs` from the packets and the synthesis outputs; do not edit by hand. Edit a packet's `02-questions.md` (through its owner) or a synthesis input, then re-run:

```
node C:/Users/Pinda/Proyectos/Biotech/biotech-knowledge-services-S/schema/program/final/_work/questions-catalog.mjs
```

Paths are relative to `schema/program/`. Every number on this page is computed by the script from the inputs listed at the end (with their sha256 prefixes).

## Header

- **Distinct questions:** 454, from 475 question rows in 14 packets (453 distinct packet ids). 21 questions are asked by two or more packets and have one row here; CQ-PF-03 is split into CQ-PF-03a (D8) and CQ-PF-03b (D6) per R-08.
- **Value:** 1 essential 154 · 2 foundational 249 · 3 expansion 51. A shared id carries the lowest value any packet declares (R-08).
- **Polarity:** harm 223 · benefit 21 · neutral 210. **Harm share 49.1%** (directive floor: at least a third of the rows). A shared id counts as harm if any packet declares harm.
- **Answer kind (D10 ruling, per question; first packet's ruling for shared ids):** retrieved 331 · materialized 20 · assessment 84 · published 19. 6 retrieved questions also have a published variant. 17 questions carry a D10 ruling that differs from a packet's declaration (⚑, adopted by R-50).
- **Answer kind per packet row (475 rows):** declared retrieved 336 · materialized 26 · assessment 89 · published 24; D10 ruling retrieved 345 · materialized 20 · assessment 90 · published 20.
- **Fixture:** 324 questions have a fixture (a packet row says yes), 130 do not. 12 declared yes but no fixture `questions.md` names the id; 37 declared no but a fixture `questions.md` names the id (both lists under "Data checks"; the packet's column is what this catalog reports).
- **Survived synthesis:** kept, typed edge(s) after synthesis 388 · kept, but a pair it cited was cut 20 · kept, pattern rerouted to another record 2 · kept, no typed edge ever (kernel records, predicates, properties) 43 · only typed edge(s) cut 1.
- **Patterns (FU-04; `final/_work/pattern-lint.json` and `final/_work/by-question/*.json`):** questions with a valid pattern 452 / 454 (value 1: 152 / 154). A pattern is valid when no packet row of the question is packetBug or rulingBroken in the lint, or when the broken row has a final-SDL rewrite in `final/patterns-by-question/` that validated ok (or gds-unvalidated); a question that only a `final/query-patterns.md` section names also counts. Validated final-SDL rewrites: 74 questions. **Pattern broken** (a packet pattern cannot return rows against the assembled SDL and has no validated rewrite): 2 (Q-D12-03 v1, Q-D12-06 v1). No pattern found: 0.
- **Pattern reference:** 68 questions are covered by a section of `final/query-patterns.md`; the rest cite their packet's `03-retrieval-patterns.md`.

### How the columns are read

- **id(s):** the catalog id; when it differs from the packet id (the R-08 split) the packet id follows. **Packets** lists every packet that asks the question; the first is the row whose wording is shown.
- **kind:** `declared → D10 ruling`; a bare kind means the two agree. ⚑ marks a disagreement; R-50 adopts the D10 ruling. *(+ published variant)* means the default answer is retrieved and a signed Report{COMPARISON} or avoidance Report is the published variant.
- **pattern:** `final/patterns-by-question/<P>.md#<id>` first when the packet pattern was broken by a ruling and its final-SDL rewrite validated; then `final/query-patterns.md#section` when the canonical pattern file covers the question, else `packets/<P>/03-retrieval-patterns.md#<heading>`.
- **fixture:** the packet's own fixture column (yes when any packet asking it says yes); the master table also names the fixture folders whose `questions.md` cite the id.
- **survived:** *yes* when the question keeps a typed edge (cited by `final/relationship-register.yaml` or a live row of `final/seams.yaml`) or never had one; *partial (R-xx)* when one pair it cited was cut but other typed edges remain; *rerouted (R-xx)* when its pattern moved to another record by ruling; ***CUT* R-xx** when every typed edge it cited was cut (the ruling names the predicate fallback, see "Synthesis effects").

### Matching (how duplicates were found)

1. **Same id** in several packets is one question (shared catalog `CQ-*` ids): 21 questions. Value is the lowest declared (R-08), audiences are the union, polarity is harm if any packet says harm, answer kind is shown per packet. CQ-PF-03 is the exception: D8 and D6 ask different things under one id, so R-08 splits it into CQ-PF-03a (D8) and CQ-PF-03b (D6).
2. **Declared reuse:** a row whose question cell names another packet's row id ("reuses CQ-…") is cross-referenced, not merged, because the reusing row bundles several catalog questions or adds facets: 23 links (listed under "Linked questions").
3. **Text similarity:** word-set Jaccard ≥ 0.3 between rows of different packets with different ids: 6 pairs, each read; 6 kept separate with a reason, 0 not yet reviewed (listed under "Similar wording").

## By audience

Each question appears under every audience it is tagged with. Within an audience: value, then harm before benefit before neutral, then domain order.

## Synthesis effects on questions

### Questions that lost a typed edge (23)

Read from `final/seams.yaml` `cutEdges` (pairs cut, with their questions and ruling; the ruling of a cut row without one is taken from its seam row status). *Typed edges after synthesis* = relationship types whose entry in `final/relationship-register.yaml` or whose live (not cut) row in `final/seams.yaml` `seams` cites the question. *Predicate routes* = `predicateSeams` rows and `predicateOnly` entries (their `promoteWhen`) that cite it.
